  "old_string": "//! `desc` subcommand — print each commit in a revision range with\n//! its full description (changeID + commitID + bookmarks + title,\n//! then the body), the anchor commit bolded.\n//!\n//! - `DescArgs`: clap surface; flattens\n//!   `options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs`.\n//! - `desc(&DescArgs)`: the op — `resolve_spec` / `resolve_header` /\n//!   `for_each_repo` + `format_commit_full` + `indent_body`.\n\nuse clap::Args;\nuse jj_lib::repo::Repo;\nuse log::{debug, info};\n\nuse crate::common;\nuse crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;\n\n/// CLI args for `desc` — just the shared read-only commit-query args.\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct DescArgs {\n    #[command(flatten)]\n    pub common: CommonArgs,\n}\n\n/// Print each commit in the resolved range with its full description.\npub fn desc(args: &DescArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"desc: enter\");\n    let c = &args.common;\n    let spec = common::resolve_spec(c.pos_rev.as_deref(), c.pos_count, &c.revision, c.limit, \"@\");\n    let hdr = common::resolve_header(&c.label, c.no_label);\n    let repos = c.resolve_repos()?;\n\n    common::for_each_repo(&repos, &hdr, |workspace, repo| {\n        let (ids, anchor_index) =\n            common::collect_ids(workspace, repo, &spec.rev, spec.desc_count, spec.anc_count)?;\n\n        for (i, commit_id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {\n            let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;\n            let bookmarks = common::format_bookmarks_at(repo, commit_id);\n            let line = common::format_commit_full(&commit, &bookmarks);\n            let indented = common::indent_body(&line, 4);\n            if i == anchor_index {\n                info!(\"{}\", common::bold_first_line(&indented));\n            } else {\n                info!(\"{indented}\");\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    })?;\n    debug!(\"desc: exit\");\n    Ok(())\n}",
  "new_string": "//! `desc` subcommand — print each commit in a revision range with\n//! its full description (changeID + commitID + bookmarks + title,\n//! then the body), the anchor commit bolded.\n//!\n//! - `DescArgs`: clap surface; flattens\n//!   `options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs`.\n//! - `DescParams`: clap-free; embeds `common::CommonParams`. Built\n//!   via `TryFrom<&DescArgs>` at the binary edge.\n//! - `desc(&Context, &DescParams)`: the op — `for_each_repo` +\n//!   `format_commit_full` + `indent_body`.\n\nuse clap::Args;\nuse jj_lib::repo::Repo;\nuse log::{debug, info};\n\nuse crate::common::{self, CommonParams};\nuse crate::context::Context;\nuse crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;\n\n/// CLI args for `desc` — just the shared read-only commit-query args.\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct DescArgs {\n    #[command(flatten)]\n    pub common: CommonArgs,\n}\n\n/// Clap-free params for `desc`; embeds resolved `CommonParams`.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct DescParams {\n    pub common: CommonParams,\n}\n\nimpl TryFrom<&DescArgs> for DescParams {\n    type Error = String;\n\n    /// Resolve clap `DescArgs` into `DescParams` by delegating to\n    /// `CommonParams::try_from`; `desc` has no fields beyond\n    /// `CommonArgs`.\n    fn try_from(a: &DescArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {\n        Ok(DescParams {\n            common: CommonParams::try_from(&a.common)?,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/// Print each commit in the resolved range with its full description.\n///\n/// `_ctx` is unused (desc has no user-config or `--log` consumer);\n/// it's present for the uniform subcommand-layer signature.\npub fn desc(_ctx: &Context, params: &DescParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"desc: enter\");\n    let c = &params.common;\n    common::for_each_repo(&c.repos, &c.header, |workspace, repo| {\n        let (ids, anchor_index) =\n            common::collect_ids(workspace, repo, &c.spec.rev, c.spec.desc_count, c.spec.anc_count)?;\n        for (i, commit_id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {\n            let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;\n            let bookmarks = common::format_bookmarks_at(repo, commit_id);\n            let line = common::format_commit_full(&commit, &bookmarks);\n            let indented = common::indent_body(&line, 4);\n            if i == anchor_index {\n                info!(\"{}\", common::bold_first_line(&indented));\n            } else {\n                info!(\"{indented}\");\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    })?;\n    debug!(\"desc: exit\");\n    Ok(())\n}"
}

Not a layering inversion:\n  `CommonParams` is the resolved/domain side, `CommonArgs` is\n  the CLI-surface side, and the conversion is a one-way edge\n  from CLI to domain. Defining the impl next to `CommonParams`\n  (the target) is the natural place.\n\n## refactor: desc → Context+Params (0.49.0-4)\n\nSecond of the four Context+Params ports; uses the shared\n`CommonParams` introduced in `-3`. Mechanical follow-through —\nno new design, same shape as chid (no fields beyond `CommonArgs`,\nso `DescParams` is just a `CommonParams` wrapper).\n\n- `desc::DescParams`: flat struct embedding `CommonParams`,\n  nothing else. `impl TryFrom<&DescArgs>` delegates to\n  `CommonParams::try_from`.\n- `pub fn desc(_ctx: &Context, params: &DescParams)` — `ctx`\n  unused (uniform-signature placeholder).\n- `main.rs` dispatch builds `Context` + `DescParams` (matches\n  the `-3` Chid arm verbatim, modulo the type names).\n- Tests: existing `DescArgs` parse tests untouched; new\n  `params_from_args_defaults` exercises the boundary resolution.\n\n# References"
}
```

1	//! `list` subcommand — list commits in a jj repo, one per line:
2	//! changeID + ochid (padded) + bookmarks + title, the anchor bolded.
3	//!
4	//! - `ListArgs`: clap surface; flattens
5	//!   `options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs` plus a `-w`/`--width`
6	//!   for the ochid column.
7	//! - `list(&ListArgs)`: the op — `resolve_spec` / `resolve_header` /
8	//!   `for_each_repo` + `format_commit_with_ochid`.
9	
10	use clap::Args;
11	use jj_lib::repo::Repo;
12	use log::{debug, info, trace, warn};
13	
14	use crate::common;
15	use crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;
16	
17	/// CLI args for `list` — the shared read-only commit-query args plus
18	/// the ochid column width.
19	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
20	pub struct ListArgs {
21	    #[command(flatten)]
22	    pub common: CommonArgs,
23	
24	    /// ochid column width
25	    #[arg(short = 'w', long = "width", default_value_t = DEFAULT_OCHID_WIDTH)]
26	    pub width: usize,
27	}
28	
29	const DEFAULT_OCHID_WIDTH: usize = 21;
30	
31	/// List commits in the resolved range with the ochid column.
32	pub fn list(args: &ListArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
33	    debug!("list: enter");
34	    let c = &args.common;
35	    let spec = common::resolve_spec(c.pos_rev.as_deref(), c.pos_count, &c.revision, c.limit, "@");
36	    trace!(
37	        "list: spec rev={} desc={:?} anc={:?}",
38	        spec.rev, spec.desc_count, spec.anc_count
39	    );
40	    let hdr = common::resolve_header(&c.label, c.no_label);
41	    let repos = c.resolve_repos()?;
42	
43	    common::for_each_repo(&repos, &hdr, |workspace, repo| {
44	        let (ids, anchor_index) =
45	            common::collect_ids(workspace, repo, &spec.rev, spec.desc_count, spec.anc_count)?;
46	        debug!("list: {} commits, anchor at {anchor_index}", ids.len());
47	
48	        if ids.is_empty() {
49	            warn!("list: no commits found for revision '{}'", spec.rev);
50	        }
51	
52	        for (i, commit_id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {
53	            let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
54	            let bookmarks = common::format_bookmarks_at(repo, commit_id);
55	            let line = common::format_commit_with_ochid(&commit, args.width, &bookmarks);
56	            if i == anchor_index {
57	                info!("{}", common::bold(&line));
58	            } else {
59	                info!("{line}");
60	            }
61	        }
62	        Ok(())
63	    })?;
64	    debug!("list: exit");
65	    Ok(())
66	}
67	
68	#[cfg(test)]
69	mod tests {
70	    use std::path::PathBuf;
71	
72	    use clap::Parser;
73	
74	    use crate::{Cli, Commands};
75	
76	    fn parse(args: &[&str]) -> super::ListArgs {
77	        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from(args).unwrap();
78	        match cli.command {
79	            Commands::List(a) => a,
80	            _ => panic!("expected List"),
81	        }
82	    }
83	
84	    #[test]
85	    fn defaults() {
86	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "list"]);
87	        assert_eq!(args.common.revision, "@");
88	        assert_eq!(args.common.repo, None);
89	        assert_eq!(args.common.scope, None);
90	        assert!(args.common.limit.is_none());
91	        assert_eq!(args.width, super::DEFAULT_OCHID_WIDTH);
92	    }
93	
94	    #[test]
95	    fn with_revision() {
96	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "list", "-r", "@-"]);
97	        assert_eq!(args.common.revision, "@-");
98	    }
99	
100	    #[test]
101	    fn with_repo() {
102	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "list", "-R", "/some/path"]);
103	        assert_eq!(args.common.repo, Some(PathBuf::from("/some/path")));
104	    }
105	
106	    #[test]
107	    fn with_scope_bot() {
108	        use crate::scope::{Scope, Side};
109	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "list", "-s", "bot"]);
110	        assert_eq!(args.common.scope, Some(Scope::Roles(vec![Side::Bot])));
111	    }
112	
113	    #[test]
114	    fn with_limit() {
115	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "list", "-n", "5"]);
116	        assert_eq!(args.common.limit, Some(5));
117	    }
118	
119	    #[test]
120	    fn all_opts() {
121	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "list", "-r", "all()", "-R", ".claude", "-n", "10"]);
122	        assert_eq!(args.common.revision, "all()");
123	        assert_eq!(args.common.repo, Some(PathBuf::from(".claude")));
124	        assert_eq!(args.common.limit, Some(10));
125	    }
126	
127	    #[test]
128	    fn positional_rev() {
129	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "list", "@-"]);
130	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_rev, Some("@-".to_string()));
131	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_count, None);
132	    }
133	
134	    #[test]
135	    fn positional_rev_and_count() {
136	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "list", "@..", "5"]);
137	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_rev, Some("@..".to_string()));
138	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_count, Some(5));
139	    }
140	
141	    #[test]
142	    fn positional_both_dots() {
143	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "list", "..abcd..", "3"]);
144	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_rev, Some("..abcd..".to_string()));
145	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_count, Some(3));
146	    }
147	
148	    #[test]
149	    fn custom_width() {
150	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "list", "-w", "30"]);
151	        assert_eq!(args.width, 30);
152	    }
153	}

  "new_string": "//! `list` subcommand — list commits in a jj repo, one per line:\n//! changeID + ochid (padded) + bookmarks + title, the anchor bolded.\n//!\n//! - `ListArgs`: clap surface; flattens\n//!   `options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs` plus a `-w`/`--width`\n//!   for the ochid column.\n//! - `ListParams`: clap-free; embeds `common::CommonParams` + `width`.\n//!   Built via `TryFrom<&ListArgs>` at the binary edge.\n//! - `list(&Context, &ListParams)`: the op — `for_each_repo` +\n//!   `format_commit_with_ochid`.\n\nuse clap::Args;\nuse jj_lib::repo::Repo;\nuse log::{debug, info, trace, warn};\n\nuse crate::common::{self, CommonParams};\nuse crate::context::Context;\nuse crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;\n\n/// CLI args for `list` — the shared read-only commit-query args plus\n/// the ochid column width.\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct ListArgs {\n    #[command(flatten)]\n    pub common: CommonArgs,\n\n    /// ochid column width\n    #[arg(short = 'w', long = \"width\", default_value_t = DEFAULT_OCHID_WIDTH)]\n    pub width: usize,\n}\n\nconst DEFAULT_OCHID_WIDTH: usize = 21;\n\n/// Clap-free params for `list`; embeds `CommonParams` and the ochid\n/// column width.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct ListParams {\n    pub common: CommonParams,\n    pub width: usize,\n}\n\nimpl TryFrom<&ListArgs> for ListParams {\n    type Error = String;\n\n    /// Resolve clap `ListArgs` into `ListParams`: delegate to\n    /// `CommonParams::try_from` for the shared fields; copy `width`\n    /// straight over (clap-applied default already resolved).\n    fn try_from(a: &ListArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {\n        Ok(ListParams {\n            common: CommonParams::try_from(&a.common)?,\n            width: a.width,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/// List commits in the resolved range with the ochid column.\n///\n/// `_ctx` is unused (list has no user-config or `--log` consumer);\n/// it's present for the uniform subcommand-layer signature.\npub fn list(_ctx: &Context, params: &ListParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"list: enter\");\n    let c = &params.common;\n    trace!(\n        \"list: spec rev={} desc={:?} anc={:?}\",\n        c.spec.rev, c.spec.desc_count, c.spec.anc_count\n    );\n\n    common::for_each_repo(&c.repos, &c.header, |workspace, repo| {\n        let (ids, anchor_index) =\n            common::collect_ids(workspace, repo, &c.spec.rev, c.spec.desc_count, c.spec.anc_count)?;\n        debug!(\"list: {} commits, anchor at {anchor_index}\", ids.len());\n\n        if ids.is_empty() {\n            warn!(\"list: no commits found for revision '{}'\", c.spec.rev);\n        }\n\n        for (i, commit_id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {\n            let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;\n            let bookmarks = common::format_bookmarks_at(repo, commit_id);\n            let line = common::format_commit_with_ochid(&commit, params.width, &bookmarks);\n            if i == anchor_index {\n                info!(\"{}\", common::bold(&line));\n            } else {\n                info!(\"{line}\");\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    })?;\n    debug!(\"list: exit\");\n    Ok(())\n}"
}
```

```
1	//! `show` subcommand — for each commit in a revision range, print
2	//! author / committer / ids / parents / children / branches /
3	//! follows / precedes / description body / a changed-files summary.
4	//!
5	//! - `ShowArgs`: clap surface; flattens
6	//!   `options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs` plus a `-f`/`--files`
7	//!   cap parsed into [`FileLimit`].
8	//! - `show(&ShowArgs)`: the op — `resolve_spec` / `resolve_header` /
9	//!   `for_each_repo`, one `show_one_commit` per commit.
10	
11	use std::sync::Arc;
12	
13	use chrono::Offset;
14	use clap::Args;
15	use jj_lib::backend::TreeValue;
16	use jj_lib::commit::Commit;
17	use jj_lib::matchers::EverythingMatcher;
18	use jj_lib::merged_tree::TreeDiffIterator;
19	use jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;
20	use jj_lib::repo::{ReadonlyRepo, Repo};
21	use jj_lib::workspace::Workspace;
22	use pollster::FutureExt;
23	
24	use log::{debug, info};
25	
26	use crate::common;
27	use crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;
28	
29	/// Parsed file limit: None (suppress), Some(n) (cap at n), or all.
30	#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
31	pub enum FileLimit {
32	    /// Don't show changed files section at all.
33	    None,
34	    /// Show up to N files (with first/last split if truncated).
35	    Cap(usize),
36	    /// Show all files.
37	    All,
38	}
39	
40	impl FileLimit {
41	    fn parse(s: &str) -> Result<Self, String> {
42	        match s {
43	            "0" => Ok(FileLimit::None),
44	            "all" => Ok(FileLimit::All),
45	            _ => s
46	                .parse::<usize>()
47	                .map(FileLimit::Cap)
48	                .map_err(|_| format!("invalid file limit '{s}': expected number, 0, or 'all'")),
49	        }
50	    }
51	}
52	
53	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
54	pub struct ShowArgs {
55	    #[command(flatten)]
56	    pub common: CommonArgs,
57	
58	    /// Max changed files: number, 0 (none), or 'all'
59	    #[arg(short = 'f', long = "files", default_value = "50")]
60	    pub files: String,
61	}
62	
63	/// Print full details + a changed-files summary for each commit in
64	/// the resolved range.
65	pub fn show(args: &ShowArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
66	    debug!("show: enter");
67	    let file_limit = FileLimit::parse(&args.files)?;
68	    let c = &args.common;
69	
70	    let spec = common::resolve_spec(c.pos_rev.as_deref(), c.pos_count, &c.revision, c.limit, "@");
71	    let hdr = common::resolve_header(&c.label, c.no_label);
72	    let repos = c.resolve_repos()?;
73	
74	    common::for_each_repo(&repos, &hdr, |workspace, repo| {
75	        let (ids, anchor_index) =
76	            common::collect_ids(workspace, repo, &spec.rev, spec.desc_count, spec.anc_count)?;
77	
78	        let mut first = true;
79	        for (i, commit_id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {
80	            if !first {
81	                info!("────────────────────────────────────────");
82	            }
83	            first = false;
84	
85	            let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
86	            show_one_commit(&commit, workspace, repo, file_limit, i == anchor_index)?;
87	        }
88	        Ok(())
89	    })?;
90	    debug!("show: exit");
91	    Ok(())
92	}
93	
94	fn show_one_commit(
95	    commit: &Commit,
96	    workspace: &Workspace,
97	    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
98	    file_limit: FileLimit,
99	    is_primary: bool,
100	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
101	    // Author
102	    let author = commit.author();
103	    info!(
104	        "Author:    {} <{}> ({})",
105	        author.name,
106	        author.email,
107	        format_timestamp(&author.timestamp)
108	    );
109	
110	    // Committer
111	    let committer = commit.committer();
112	    info!(
113	        "Committer: {} <{}> ({})",
114	        committer.name,
115	        committer.email,
116	        format_timestamp(&committer.timestamp)
117	    );
118	
119	    // Ids
120	    let bookmarks = common::format_bookmarks_at(repo, commit.id());
121	    let ids_line = common::format_commit_short(commit, &bookmarks);
122	    if is_primary {
123	        info!("Ids:       {}", common::bold(&ids_line));
124	    } else {
125	        info!("Ids:       {ids_line}");
126	    }
127	
128	    // Parents
129	    let root_commit_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
130	    for parent_id in commit.parent_ids() {
131	        if *parent_id == root_commit_id {
132	            continue;
133	        }
134	        let parent = repo.store().get_commit(parent_id)?;
135	        let bm = common::format_bookmarks_at(repo, parent.id());
136	        info!("Parent:    {}", common::format_commit_short(&parent, &bm));
137	    }
138	
139	    // Children
140	    let children_ids =
141	        common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &format!("children({})", commit.id().hex()))?;
142	    for child_id in &children_ids {
143	        if *child_id == root_commit_id {
144	            continue;
145	        }
146	        let child = repo.store().get_commit(child_id)?;
147	        let bm = common::format_bookmarks_at(repo, child.id());
148	        info!("Child:     {}", common::format_commit_short(&child, &bm));
149	    }
150	
151	    // Branches
152	    let branches_str = format_branches(commit, workspace, repo)?;
153	    info!("Branches:  {branches_str}");
154	
155	    // Follows
156	    let follows = find_nearest_tag(commit, workspace, repo, true)?;
157	    info!("Follows:   {}", follows.as_deref().unwrap_or("")); // OK: obvious
158	
159	    // Precedes
160	    let precedes = find_nearest_tag(commit, workspace, repo, false)?;
161	    info!("Precedes:  {}", precedes.as_deref().unwrap_or("")); // OK: obvious
162	
163	    // Description (body only — title is on the Ids line)
164	    print_description(commit);
165	
166	    // Changed files
167	    if file_limit != FileLimit::None {
168	        print_diff(commit, repo, file_limit)?;
169	    }
170	
171	    Ok(())
172	}
173	
174	fn find_nearest_tag(
175	    commit: &Commit,
176	    workspace: &Workspace,
177	    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
178	    ancestor: bool,
179	) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
180	    let commit_hex = commit.id().hex();
181	
182	    let revset_str = if ancestor {
183	        format!("heads(tags() & ::{})", commit_hex)
184	    } else {
185	        format!("roots(tags() & {}::)", commit_hex)
186	    };
187	
188	    let tag_ids = common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &revset_str)?;
189	    if tag_ids.is_empty() {
190	        return Ok(None);
191	    }
192	
193	    let tag_id = &tag_ids[0];
194	    let view = repo.view();
195	    for (name, local_remote) in view.tags() {
196	        if local_remote.local_target.added_ids().any(|id| id == tag_id) {
197	            return Ok(Some(name.as_str().to_string()));
198	        }
199	    }
200	
201	    Ok(None)
202	}
203	
204	fn format_branches(
205	    commit: &Commit,
206	    workspace: &Workspace,
207	    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
208	) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
209	    let commit_hex = commit.id().hex();
210	    let view = repo.view();
211	    let mut names: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
212	
213	    for (name, local_remote) in view.bookmarks() {
214	        for bookmark_id in local_remote.local_target.added_ids() {
215	            let revset_str = format!("{} & ::{}", commit_hex, bookmark_id.hex());
216	            let results = common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &revset_str)?;
217	            if !results.is_empty() {
218	                names.push(name.as_str().to_string());
219	                break;
220	            }
221	        }
222	
223	        for (remote_name, remote_ref) in &local_remote.remote_refs {
224	            if remote_name.as_str() == "git" {
225	                continue;
226	            }
227	            for bookmark_id in remote_ref.target.added_ids() {
228	                let revset_str = format!("{} & ::{}", commit_hex, bookmark_id.hex());
229	                let results = common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &revset_str)?;
230	                if !results.is_empty() {
231	                    names.push(format!("{}@{}", name.as_str(), remote_name.as_str()));
232	                    break;
233	                }
234	            }
235	        }
236	    }
237	
238	    Ok(names.join(", "))
239	}
240	
241	fn format_timestamp(ts: &jj_lib::backend::Timestamp) -> String {
242	    let millis = ts.timestamp.0;
243	    let secs = millis / 1000;
244	    let tz_minutes = ts.tz_offset;
245	
246	    let dt = chrono::DateTime::from_timestamp(secs, 0)
247	        .unwrap_or_default() // OK: invalid timestamp → epoch for display
248	        .with_timezone(
249	            &chrono::FixedOffset::east_opt(tz_minutes * 60).unwrap_or(chrono::Utc.fix()), // OK: invalid tz → UTC for display
250	        );
251	
252	    dt.format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S").to_string()
253	}
254	
255	fn print_description(commit: &Commit) {
256	    let desc = commit.description();
257	    let body: String = desc
258	        .lines()
259	        .skip(1)
260	        .skip_while(|l| l.is_empty())
261	        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
262	        .join("\n");
263	
264	    info!("Description:");
265	    for line in body.lines() {
266	        info!("    {line}");
267	    }
268	}
269	
270	fn print_diff(
271	    commit: &Commit,
272	    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
273	    file_limit: FileLimit,
274	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
275	    let parent_tree = commit.parent_tree(repo.as_ref()).block_on()?;
276	    let commit_tree = commit.tree();
277	
278	    let diff_iter = TreeDiffIterator::new(&parent_tree, &commit_tree, &EverythingMatcher);
279	
280	    let mut lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
281	    for entry in diff_iter {
282	        let diff = entry.values?;
283	        let before = diff.before.as_resolved().cloned().flatten();
284	        let after = diff.after.as_resolved().cloned().flatten();
285	
286	        if matches!(&before, Some(TreeValue::Tree(_))) || matches!(&after, Some(TreeValue::Tree(_)))
287	        {
288	            continue;
289	        }
290	
291	        let path_str = entry.path.as_internal_file_string().to_string();
292	
293	        let line = match (before, after) {
294	            (None, Some(value)) => {
295	                format!("    Added {} {path_str}", file_type_str(&value))
296	            }
297	            (Some(_), None) => {
298	                format!(
299	                    "    Removed {} {path_str}",
300	                    file_type_str_from_merged(&diff.before),
301	                )
302	            }
303	            (Some(_), Some(value)) => {
304	                format!("    Modified {} {path_str}", file_type_str(&value))
305	            }
306	            (None, None) => continue,
307	        };
308	        lines.push(line);
309	    }
310	
311	    let total = lines.len();
312	    let cap = match file_limit {
313	        FileLimit::All | FileLimit::None => 0,
314	        FileLimit::Cap(n) => n,
315	    };
316	    let show_all = cap == 0 || total <= cap;
317	
318	    if show_all {
319	        info!("Changed files: {total}");
320	        for line in &lines {
321	            info!("{line}");
322	        }
323	    } else {
324	        info!("Changed files: {total}, showing first {cap}");
325	        for line in &lines[..cap] {
326	            info!("{line}");
327	        }
328	    }
329	
330	    Ok(())
331	}
332	
333	fn file_type_str(value: &TreeValue) -> &'static str {
334	    match value {
335	        TreeValue::File {
336	            executable: false, ..
337	        } => "regular file",
338	        TreeValue::File {
339	            executable: true, ..
340	        } => "executable file",
341	        TreeValue::Symlink(_) => "symlink",
342	        TreeValue::Tree(_) => "tree",
343	        TreeValue::GitSubmodule(_) => "git submodule",
344	    }
345	}
346	
347	fn file_type_str_from_merged(merged: &jj_lib::merge::Merge<Option<TreeValue>>) -> &'static str {
348	    if let Some(Some(value)) = merged.as_resolved() {
349	        file_type_str(value)
350	    } else {
351	        "file"
352	    }
353	}
354	
355	#[cfg(test)]
356	mod tests {
357	    use std::path::PathBuf;
358	
359	    use clap::Parser;
360	
361	    use crate::{Cli, Commands};
362	
363	    fn parse(args: &[&str]) -> super::ShowArgs {
364	        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from(args).unwrap();
365	        match cli.command {
366	            Commands::Show(a) => a,
367	            _ => panic!("expected Show"),
368	        }
369	    }
370	
371	    #[test]
372	    fn defaults() {
373	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show"]);
374	        assert_eq!(args.common.revision, "@");
375	        assert_eq!(args.common.repo, None);
376	        assert_eq!(args.common.scope, None);
377	        assert_eq!(args.files, "50");
378	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_rev, None);
379	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_count, None);
380	        assert_eq!(args.common.limit, None);
381	    }
382	
383	    #[test]
384	    fn with_revision() {
385	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-r", "@-"]);
386	        assert_eq!(args.common.revision, "@-");
387	    }
388	
389	    #[test]
390	    fn with_repo() {
391	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-R", ".claude"]);
392	        assert_eq!(args.common.repo, Some(PathBuf::from(".claude")));
393	    }
394	
395	    #[test]
396	    fn with_scope_code() {

398	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-s", "code"]);
399	        assert_eq!(args.common.scope, Some(Scope::Roles(vec![Side::Code])));
400	    }
401	
402	    #[test]
403	    fn positional_rev() {
404	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "@-"]);
405	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_rev, Some("@-".to_string()));
406	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_count, None);
407	    }
408	
409	    #[test]

411	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "@..", "3"]);
412	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_rev, Some("@..".to_string()));
413	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_count, Some(3));
414	    }
415	
416	    #[test]

419	        assert_eq!(args.files, "0");

438	        ]);

441	        assert_eq!(args.files, "100");

`show(&ShowArgs)`: the op — `resolve_spec` / `resolve_header` /\n//!   `for_each_repo`, one `show_one_commit` per commit.\n\nuse std::sync::Arc;\n\nuse chrono::Offset;\nuse clap::Args;\nuse jj_lib::backend::TreeValue;\nuse jj_lib::commit::Commit;\nuse jj_lib::matchers::EverythingMatcher;\nuse jj_lib::merged_tree::TreeDiffIterator;\nuse jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;\nuse parse(s: &str) -> Result<Self, String> {\n        match s {\n            \"0\" => Ok(FileLimit::None),\n            \"all\" => Ok(FileLimit::All),\n            _ => s\n                .parse::<usize>()\n                .map(FileLimit::Cap)\n                .map_err(|_| format!(\"invalid file limit '{s}': expected number, 0, or 'all'\")),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct ShowArgs {\n    #[command(flatten)]\n    pub common: CommonArgs,\n\n    /// Max changed files: number, 0 (none), or 'all'\n    #[arg(short = 'f', long = \"files\", default_value = \"50\")]\n    pub files: String,\n}\n\n/// Print full details + a changed-files summary for each commit in\n/// the resolved range.\npub fn show(args: &ShowArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"show: enter\");\n    let file_limit = FileLimit::parse(&args.files)?;\n    let c = &args.common;\n\n    let spec = common::resolve_spec(c.pos_rev.as_deref(), c.pos_count, &c.revision, c.limit, \"@\");\n    let hdr = common::resolve_header(&c.label, c.no_label);\n    let repos = c.resolve_repos()?;\n\n    common::for_each_repo(&repos, &hdr, |workspace, repo| {\n        let (ids, anchor_index) =\n            common::collect_ids(workspace, repo, &spec.rev, spec.desc_count, spec.anc_count)?;\n\n        let mut first = true;\n        for (i, commit_id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {\n            if !first {\n                info!(\"────────────────────────────────────────\");\n            }\n            first = false;\n\n            let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;\n            show_one_commit(&commit, workspace, repo, file_limit, i == anchor_index)?;\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    })?;\n    debug!(\"show: exit\");\n    Ok(())\n}",
  "new_string": "//! `show` subcommand — for each commit in a revision range, print\n//! author / committer / ids / parents / children / branches /\n//! follows / precedes / description body / a changed-files summary.\n//!\n//! - `ShowArgs`: clap surface; flattens\n//!   `options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs` plus a `-f`/`--files`\n//!   cap (raw `String`; parsed at the boundary into [`FileLimit`]).\n//! - `ShowParams`: clap-free; embeds `common::CommonParams` and the\n//!   parsed `FileLimit`. Built via `TryFrom<&ShowArgs>` at the\n//!   binary edge.\n//! - `show(&Context, &ShowParams)`: the op — `for_each_repo`, one\n//!   `show_one_commit` per commit.\n\nuse std::sync::Arc;\n\nuse chrono::Offset;\nuse clap::Args;\nuse jj_lib::backend::TreeValue;\nuse jj_lib::commit::Commit;\nuse jj_lib::matchers::EverythingMatcher;\nuse jj_lib::merged_tree::TreeDiffIterator;\nuse jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;\nuse jj_lib::repo::{ReadonlyRepo, Repo};\nuse jj_lib::workspace::Workspace;\nuse pollster::FutureExt;\n\nuse log::{debug, info};\n\nuse crate::common::{self, CommonParams};\nuse crate::context::Context;\nuse crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;\n\n/// Parsed file limit: None (suppress), Some(n) (cap at n), or all.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum FileLimit {\n    /// Don't show changed files section at all.\n    None,\n    /// Show up to N files (with first/last split if truncated).\n    Cap(usize),\n    /// Show all files.\n    All,\n}\n\nimpl FileLimit {\n    /// Parse the `--files` flag string into a `FileLimit` — `\"0\"` →\n    /// None, `\"all\"` → All, otherwise a positive integer → Cap(n).\n    /// Called at the boundary by `ShowParams::try_from`.\n    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Result<Self, String> {\n        match s {\n            \"0\" => Ok(FileLimit::None),\n            \"all\" => Ok(FileLimit::All),\n            _ => s\n                .parse::<usize>()\n                .map(FileLimit::Cap)\n                .map_err(|_| format!(\"invalid file limit '{s}': expected number, 0, or 'all'\")),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct ShowArgs {\n    #[command(flatten)]\n    pub common: CommonArgs,\n\n    /// Max changed files: number, 0 (none), or 'all'\n    #[arg(short = 'f', long = \"files\", default_value = \"50\")]\n    pub files: String,\n}\n\n/// Clap-free params for `show`; embeds `CommonParams` plus the parsed\n/// `FileLimit`.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct ShowParams {\n    pub common: CommonParams,\n    pub files: FileLimit,\n}\n\nimpl TryFrom<&ShowArgs> for ShowParams {\n    type Error = String;\n\n    /// Resolve clap `ShowArgs` into `ShowParams`: delegate to\n    /// `CommonParams::try_from` for the shared fields and parse\n    /// `--files` into `FileLimit` at the boundary. Fallible for two\n    /// reasons (`resolve_repos` or `FileLimit::parse`); both errors\n    /// surface as `String` for uniform handling in `main`.\n    fn try_from(a: &ShowArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {\n        Ok(ShowParams {\n            common: CommonParams::try_from(&a.common)?,\n            files: FileLimit::parse(&a.files)?,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/// Print full details + a changed-files summary for each commit in\n/// the resolved range.\n///\n/// `_ctx` is unused (show has no user-config or `--log` consumer);\n/// it's present for the uniform subcommand-layer signature.\npub fn show(_ctx: &Context, params: &ShowParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    debug!(\"show: enter\");\n    let c = &params.common;\n\n    common::for_each_repo(&c.repos, &c.header, |workspace, repo| {\n        let (ids, anchor_index) =\n            common::collect_ids(workspace, repo, &c.spec.rev, c.spec.desc_count, c.spec.anc_count)?;\n\n        let mut first = true;\n        for (i, commit_id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {\n            if !first {\n                info!(\"────────────────────────────────────────\");\n            }\n            first = false;\n\n            let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;\n            show_one_commit(&commit, workspace, repo, params.files, i == anchor_index)?;\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    })?;\n    debug!(\"show: exit\");\n    Ok(())\n}"
}

  "new_string": "    #[test]\n    fn all_opts() {\n        let args = parse(&[\n            \"vc-x1\", \"show\", \"-r\", \"@--\", \"-R\", \"/tmp\", \"-f\", \"100\", \"-n\", \"3\",\n        ]);\n        assert_eq!(args.common.revision, \"@--\");\n        assert_eq!(args.common.repo, Some(PathBuf::from(\"/tmp\")));\n        assert_eq!(args.files, \"100\");\n        assert_eq!(args.common.limit, Some(3));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn params_from_args_defaults() {\n        // ShowParams::try_from goes through the binary-edge resolution\n        // and parses --files into a FileLimit. Default \"50\" → Cap(50).\n        use super::{FileLimit, ShowParams};\n        let args = parse(&[\"vc-x1\", \"show\"]);\n        let params = ShowParams::try_from(&args).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(params.common.repos, vec![PathBuf::from(\".\")]);\n        assert_eq!(params.common.spec.rev, \"@\");\n        assert_eq!(params.files, FileLimit::Cap(50));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn params_from_args_files_variants() {\n        use super::{FileLimit, ShowParams};\n        let args = parse(&[\"vc-x1\", \"show\", \"-f\", \"0\"]);\n        assert_eq!(ShowParams::try_from(&args).unwrap().files, FileLimit::None);\n        let args = parse(&[\"vc-x1\", \"show\", \"-f\", \"all\"]);\n        assert_eq!(ShowParams::try_from(&args).unwrap().files, FileLimit::All);\n        let args = parse(&[\"vc-x1\", \"show\", \"-f\", \"5\"]);\n        assert_eq!(\n            ShowParams::try_from(&args).unwrap().files,\n            FileLimit::Cap(5)\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn params_from_args_files_invalid() {\n        // FileLimit::parse error surfaces through ShowParams::try_from.\n        use super::ShowParams;\n        let args = parse(&[\"vc-x1\", \"show\", \"-f\", \"bogus\"]);\n        let err = ShowParams::try_from(&args).unwrap_err();\n        assert!(err.contains(\"invalid file limit\"), \"got: {err}\");\n    }\n}"
}

> AGENT

`-6` work complete. Dogfood: `vc-x1 show -r @- -s code,bot -f 3` prints full Author/Ids/Parents/etc. across both repos — full parity.

Fourth and final Context+Params port; completes Migration A at **12/12**. Show's wrinkle is that `--files` is a `String` on the args side and parses into `FileLimit` at the boundary — so `ShowParams::try_from` is fallible for *two* reasons (`resolve_repos` + `FileLimit::parse`), both surfacing as `String`.

- **`src/show.rs`** — new `ShowParams { common: CommonParams, files: FileLimit }`; `impl TryFrom<&ShowArgs>` delegates `common` and calls `FileLimit::parse` at the boundary (the parse step moves out of `pub fn show`); `FileLimit::parse` widened from `fn` to `pub fn` so the boundary call can reach it; `pub fn show` ported to `(_ctx: &Context, params: &ShowParams)`; three new tests (`params_from_args_defaults`, `params_from_args_files_variants`, `params_from_args_files_invalid`).
- **`src/main.rs`** — `Commands::Show` arm builds `Context` + `ShowParams` (same shape as `-3` / `-4` / `-5`).
- **`Cargo.toml` / `Cargo.lock`** — bump `0.49.0-5` → `0.49.0-6`.
- **`notes/chores-10.md`** — backfill `-5`'s `Commits:` ref ([[10]]); flip Refactor tracking (`show` → done — every row now reads "done"); add `## refactor: show → Context+Params (0.49.0-6)` section.
- **`notes/todo.md`** — `-6` marked `(done)`.

Cargo cycle clean. Please review.

**Next up after this push:** the `0.49.0` close-out (drop the suffix; move the `0.49.0` In-Progress entry to Done; sweep README + ARCHITECTURE.md per the ladder).

166	## args → Context + Params
167	
168	The ongoing port — "the Context+Params port" — of each
169	subcommand's `pub fn x(args: &XxxArgs)` to
170	`pub fn x(ctx: &Context, params: &XxxParams)`, adding an
171	`XxxParams` flat struct + a `From` (or `TryFrom`, if the
172	conversion is fallible) at the binary edge. `init` (0.44.0) is

176	fields beyond `UserConfig` + `--log`. Per-subcommand status:

179	## per-subcommand flags → `src/options_flags/`
180

185	[`src/options_flags/README.md`](src/options_flags/README.md)

3	This directory contains various notes and documentation related to the project.

6	By default there are chores-*.md and todo.md. Chores are general notes
